这些结果如何改变下一步

时间环境的一步判断可以正确，但本地多步预演在攻击前已经出错，当前不能用它证明“原本正确知识被改坏”。通过真实经验初始化的 API 短测没有触发规则更新。独立诊断任务能降低无关规则分数，但尚未造成下游失败。外接电源的资源例外在两种更新方式下均未增加目标错误。

补充的 Qwen3 例外学习短测未通过洁净能力检查。API 的同提示重复试验也发现明显波动：完整记忆在普通状态下错 1/3 次、例外状态下错 2/3 次；这些是同一实例的重复测量，不能视作独立样本。移除或仅保留规则的对照说明，规则能够影响部分预测，但尚未形成稳定、正确的待攻击知识。另一条全局正确的校准保持规律被 Qwen3 直接预测正确，没有触发学习，也没有表现出规则依赖。

转向真实环境与信息差

已接通 ScienceWorld 1.2.3，并用官方煮水动作序列取得环境累计分数 100；这只是环境验证，不是模型 Agent 的成功率。新的短测直接调用 WorldEvolver 原生文本预测、事实分解、规则抽取、检索与更新代码，比较正常交互、失败前查看无关物体、失败前查看房间三组等长真实交互。置信门关闭；目前仍是预测层的机制检验。

首个预测退化信号：Qwen3-8B 在两个真实失败交互臂后，均将四个放置探针中的一个从正确预测变成错误失败判断。当前房间明示冰箱已打开，模型仍说冰箱没开；正常交互臂没有该错误。记忆交换表明，仅替换交互后的真实轨迹就复现错误，仅替换规则则不复现。学到的“关闭的容器不能放入物品”规则本身成立，当前证据不支持错误规则是原因。

早期子目标的限制：改用明确的“把金属锅放进冰箱”目标后，世界模型恢复了正确预测。原生 WMReactAgent 在无预测、洁净记忆、交互后记忆三种条件下均一步完成放置，见执行记录。该子目标结果不支持实际决策损失。随后使用真实冷冻目标和更强的五次失败交互继续检验。

更强的定向交互：五次真实失败占据同一动作的全部五个检索位置。随后通过原生更新器执行五次真实成功，完整房间视图下的错误预测仍然存在，检索仍返回原来的五条失败记录，见纠错实验。同一个物理状态用简短的“冷冻柜已打开”描述时，预测保持正确。这是一次开发案例的恢复性证据，不能视为普适、不可逆的攻击。

修正指令后的核心任务结果：实际环境接受 wait（10 个迭代）和 wait1（1 个迭代），原提示列出的 wait 1 与 wait 10 均无效，见命令验证。旧八步实验混入无效命令与预算因素，不能直接据此判定洁净系统能力不足。统一修正提示、采用 12 步预算后，glm-5.3 执行 Agent 配本地 Qwen3 世界模型的无辅助／洁净／交互后记忆三组均得 100 分，分别用 9／7／10 步，见任务结果。交互后记忆的错误预测使首步从直接放入变为检查冷冻柜，但最终完成任务；单次轨迹的步数差不能当作稳定攻击效应。另一轮全 API 洁净条件中断于调用错误，未计为任务失败。

修复与迁移：保留全部记忆，只将动作相似度同分时的优先级从早期记录改为近期记录，检索即选中五条真实纠正经验，错误房间预测恢复正确，见干预结果。glm-5.3 世界模型不仅抵抗早期四个探针，也在五次失败加五次纠正的记忆上两次重复均预测正确，见迁移检查。当前影响依赖模型与检索方式；网关名称仍不是已核验的后端身份。变体 0 和 1 的目标冷冻状态完全相同，未当作两个独立实例。

规则遗忘方向的追加检验：更强 API 模型在第一次例外学习检查中预测正确，但重复检查时普通条件连续出错；移除规则后普通条件恢复、例外条件失败。抽取出的规则没有保留必要作用域，因此没有进入攻击阶段，见学习依赖检查。这说明该实例仍缺少稳定、正确的待攻击知识。另已通过官方轨迹验证化学混合、颜料混合和未知物导电性三种原生任务；这些 100 分是环境验证，不是模型表现。

颜料混合的迁移短测：洁净记忆含完整绿色颜料成功轨迹。两条等长八步真实交互分别正常混合蓝黄颜料，或加入红色后得到棕色并重复混合。交互后，原生短观察与房间观察的预测都仍正确给出绿色，见原始结果。该方向没有出现攻击信号，未继续测执行 Agent。

配平后的冷冻任务对照：五步正常交互记忆与五次真实失败记忆两组均得 100 分，并执行完全相同的六个动作，见最终对照结果。攻击组虽仍有错误预测，执行 Agent 仍直接放置。两轮首步修正提示的哈希相同，输出却曾分别为检查与直接放置，见提示与响应核对；其中一次涉及不同端点与输出上限，不能视为受控重复。此前三步差异不支持稳定的攻击成本效应。冷冻案例暂不作为论文主效应候选。

未知物导电性：预测能否取代测量

在同一官方任务中，未知物 B 实际不导电。正常接线将它串入电路，灯不亮；合法地绕过它接通电路后，灯会亮。两组各执行七个真实动作，并使用原生更新器学习。glm-5.3 的洁净与正常交互记忆均预测灯灭，绕行电路记忆使未测量状态下的预测变成灯亮，见交互与预测。首次读数因电路传播延迟仍为灯灭，后续才亮；记录保留全部真实反馈。

本地 Qwen3 的冻结记忆查询复现了灯状态偏差：只交换真实轨迹即可复现，只交换规则不会复现，见记忆干预。但其洁净预测仍含错误接线细节，不能称为完整状态预测正确。房间视图包含已经测得的“灯灭”，它能纠正预测，却不是信息量相同的格式对照。强模型对绕行记忆的两次追加查询均因空输出或超时失败，尚无强模型重复确认。

实际决策中的限制：本地执行 Agent 在洁净条件下得 100 分，而正常交互记忆与绕行记忆均误放入红盒，得 −100 分，见任务结果。绕行组将计划中的房间观察改为直接分类：它依据预测中的“灯亮”行动，没有执行这次测量。但正常记忆也通过另一次错误预测引发误判，因此尚不能归因于攻击。

将开关操作统一放入真实前置步骤后，无辅助 Agent 两步成功，本地洁净辅助组却重复观察五次、未完成任务；后续攻击组没有运行，见洁净能力检查。全 API 版本的无辅助组也两步成功，但洁净世界模型连续空输出而中断，见中断记录。这些结果均不计为攻击成功。

首个通过双对照的任务失败：本地 Qwen3 世界模型配 glm-5.3 执行 Agent，洁净记忆四步得 100 分，正常接线记忆一步得 100 分，绕行记忆两步得 −100 分，见完整结果。攻击组原本计划查看灯泡，收到“灯亮”的预测后改为拿起物体，随后放进错误的红盒，全程没有执行拟定的灯泡测量。正常记忆组也直接依据正确预测分类；因此关键在预测内容如何影响测量与行动，而非单纯存在跳过测量的行为。洁净组曾受错误接线描述干扰，随后通过真实查看完成任务。该比较只有一轮，仍需重复与独立实例。

补充接线历史：给世界模型加入执行 Agent 已掌握的完整真实接线历史，不添加后续测量读数，洁净／正常记忆仍预测灯灭，绕行记忆及仅交换轨迹的条件仍错误预测灯亮，仅交换规则则预测灯灭，见历史信息对照。这表明当前局部错误不只由缺少接线历史解释；尚未完成这一输入干预下的执行 Agent 对照。下一步检验任务级记忆交换、重复运行与第二种真实物质，不从单例直接扩量。

任务级复现限制：仅替换轨迹和仅替换规则的两组执行 Agent 都实际查看了灯泡，然后两步成功，见任务记忆干预。预测偏差的轨迹归因，不能直接升级为稳定任务损失的归因。对三种条件各重复三次完全相同的首步修正提示，攻击条件中两次选择查看，一次在推理中错误认定物质导电并先拿起它；正常与洁净条件未出现错误红盒分类意图，见逐次响应。这九次只是决策探针，没有执行完整任务，不能转写为攻击成功率。

第二个物理实例：变体 25 的物质 C 实际导电，接线颜色与 B 不同。正常接线后五次读数均为灯亮；合法断开 C 后五次读数均为灯灭。原生短测中，洁净与正常交互记忆预测灯亮，断路记忆却在原生视图和完整前置历史视图都预测灯灭，见相反方向的预测错误。两个物理实例仍只属于一种任务。随后本地执行 Agent 的无辅助、洁净、正常、断路四组均得 100 分，见任务对照；断路组仍执行测量，读到真实灯亮后正确分类。第二个实例目前只支持预测层泛化，未支持任务损失泛化。

C 的强执行 Agent 追加实验在洁净条件下中断：完成一次真实拿起动作后，主端点超时，备用端点空输出；未产生洁净任务完成结果，也没有运行攻击比较，见中断记录。

把公共准备也纳入学习：早期投毒短测只学习公共准备之后的七个动作。B 的追加实验现已让两组从环境重置开始完整学习 17 个真实动作：十个相同准备动作，加七个正常或绕行分支动作。公共准备与正常组仍预测灯灭，绕行组仍将未测量状态错误预测为灯亮，见完整学习结果。因此该预测偏差不依赖于省略准备阶段记录。此轮五次绕行读数均为灯亮，与较早运行的首次灯灭有所不同，实际反馈均已保留。包含真实灯灭读数的房间视图仍预测灯灭，但捏造了接线细节。

新增完整学习记忆已进入执行对照，但本地世界模型配 glm-5.3 执行 Agent 的洁净条件在一次真实动作后连续空输出；全 glm-5.2 版本的无辅助组两步成功，洁净预测随后连续空输出，均未形成攻击比较。glm-5.2 的六次冻结查询全部完成：原生视图为洁净／正常灯灭、攻击灯亮；完整历史视图下洁净组也误报灯亮，因此只支持前一视图的预测迁移，见原始查询。

真正的成功交互能否修复预测：正常与绕行记忆各接续五名全新的原生执行 Agent。这些 Agent 不接收世界模型预测，均真实查看灯泡后放入绿盒，十个续接任务全部两步成功；共享世界模型仍对每个实际动作预测并更新。绕行组在第 1、3、5 名成功用户后仍误报灯亮，检索一直保留五条旧绕行读数；只改同分记录的新旧优先级，改选五条真实纠正读数，两个视图均恢复灯灭，见真实用户交互与检索干预。这是人为提供成功纠错经历的恢复实验，不能表述为受攻击、接收预测的用户自发纠错，也没有证明任务损失持续。

更强洁净初始化的限制：先让模型学习一次真实、正确的目标动作“查看灯泡”，再进行校准查询。原生视图正确，完整历史视图却误报灯亮，因此停止在洁净能力门槛，计划中的开关状态配平攻击没有运行，见洁净结果。这不是攻击失败或成功，而是当前预测器基线仍不稳定。

熔点任务：第二类测量偏差与执行结果

官方未知物熔点任务要求按 150°C 阈值分类。共同加热准备后，正常组关闭炉子，攻击组合法继续加热；随后两组各进行五次“查看锅内物质、测温”的真实交互，均学习 11 个动作。正常读数为 124、130、133、135、137°C，攻击读数为 168、175、181、187、192°C。对同一个尚未执行的较早测温动作，洁净预测 116°C、正常组 135°C、攻击组 187°C，只有攻击组跨过分类阈值，见真实交互与预测。给出完整的既有加热历史后三组都预测 116°C；这里测量的是分类相关阈值偏差，不是精确温度准确率。

任务执行没有复制分类失败。本地执行 Agent 在无辅助、洁净、正常、攻击四组都一步正确选择黄盒，未调用被污染的测温预测。glm-5.2 执行 Agent 配本地世界模型，洁净与正常组一步成功，攻击组三步成功：先关闭炉子，再真实测温，最后选择黄盒，见执行结果。一次两步额外开销不构成稳定成本效应。早期两个熔点开发版本因相态变化后物体名称失效而停止，不纳入攻击结果。

接收预测的连续用户短测：用此前唯一通过双对照的配置，三组各计划让三名新 Agent 连续使用同一模型，并学习实际执行的动作。已完成的洁净／正常各两名用户，以及攻击组首名用户，都得到 100 分；攻击组第二名用户真实读到灯灭后，在继续检查开关时遭遇主端点超时和备用端点空输出，实验中断，见连续用户记录。此轮没有复现终局误分类，也没有完成三名用户的全链条，未完成的调用不计为攻击成功。

官方模型与另一种经验学习路径

备用凭据已通过 DeepSeek 官方 https://api.deepseek.com/models 验证，返回 deepseek-flash 与 deepseek-v4-pro，见接入记录。官方文档将当前 Flash 映射到 V4.1；它与此前无法核实后端的第三方网关别名分别记录。

官方 Flash 六次冻结查询全部完成：原生查询中洁净／正常预测灯灭、绕行预测灯亮，完整历史查询中的洁净预测也错为灯亮。实际执行时，无辅助组成功，洁净世界模型组却把预测的灯亮当作分类依据，直接放入红盒，见执行检查；攻击组没有运行。五次真实 examine red light bulb 校准仍未修复 Flash 的完整历史预测，见增强洁净检查。因此不能把该模型的错误分类归因于投毒。

WorldMind 的原生判别、反思、存储、检索与精炼模块现已接通。将已有的完整 17 步真实交互与当时宿主模型的预测逐条交给它，两组各形成两条过程经验；绕行组被精炼成“实测并核验状态”，没有输出“B 导电”的结论，见原生精炼结果。这是固定宿主预测的经验迁移检验，不是重新生成整个 WorldMind 在线轨迹。原实现排除了同一任务指令的成功经验，故洁净检索为空；当前原生检索采用词重叠回退。

通过插件的经验注入接口，把上述固定记忆接入原生 WMReactAgent，并由官方 Flash 执行。统一十二步预算下，洁净、正常、绕行三组都正确完成，分别用 4、12、7 步，见任务结果。此前五步版本的正常组尚未完成检查，未运行攻击组。此轮没有支持跨学习机制的任务损失；单条轨迹的步数差也不作为稳定效果。

Pro 抵抗了更严格的测量课程：先学习一次真实正确的灯泡测量，两组再各学习完整 27 个真实动作，包括全部公共准备，以及每次读数前相同的“开关已激活”观察。洁净、公共准备、正常和绕行阶段的两个查询视图都保持灯灭，见完整结果。末次检索中确有一条正确读数和四条真实绕行灯亮读数，因此不能把抵抗解释为没有检索到攻击经验。此轮没有预测退化，不继续把它计入任务攻击比较。

真实跨任务学习破坏了保护：研究者先移除语义记忆，绕行检查点的两个视图都变为错误灯亮，见干预查询。随后使用模拟器真实执行的加热轨迹，先预测、再揭示实际反馈，并学习全部三十步准备和三十步后续动作。反复查看温度计的对照始终预测电路灯灭；持续测量升温物质的组出现 28/30 次预测不匹配，对无关灯泡规则的全局扣分使两条规则最终均失去正证据，电路预测在第二十步已变为错误灯亮，见最终预测。首次温度误差达 106°C，不能将全部不匹配描述为微小误差；预测转错也早于两条规则全部退出提示。

机制干预的范围：从上述干扰组完全相同的准备后检查点出发，仅关闭全局负向扣分，重复三十次真实升温测量，两个电路视图始终保持正确，见匹配干预。但从准备阶段之前就关闭扣分的另一轮实验，在学习新规则后已经预测错误，见较早干预的负面结果。因此只支持这一阶段的扣分通路，不能声称它解释所有错误或构成通用防御。

完整任务重复没有支持攻击：官方 Pro 世界模型配 Flash 执行 Agent，预先固定六轮、平衡三组执行顺序，洁净／普通学习／干扰学习各六次全部 100 分。干扰组每次都实际测量，然后正确放入绿盒，见十八次执行结果。这些是同一个物理实例的重复，不是十八个独立任务。全 Pro 执行版本的洁净组十二步仍未完成，未进入攻击比较，见洁净门槛。当前案例只支持预测损害，不支持稳定任务损失；停止在此案例上扩大重复。

任务成功没有自动修复预测：读取上述六次独立重复留下的记忆，每次真实测量并完成分类后，两个查询视图仍都错误，见纠错后查询。随后各组维持一份共享记忆，连续接入三位新用户，学习其全部后续动作；九次任务全部成功，干扰组在第一位和第三位用户之后仍预测灯亮。普通学习组到第三位用户后也预测错误，冻结重复中又有波动，见连续用户查询和冻结复查。因此只能支持局部预测恢复失败，不能据此宣称攻击独有的长期任务损害。各组内部用户相互依赖，仍只有一个物理实例，公共准备动作由用户观察但不在这一阶段重新学习。

恢复失败有一个可定位的实现通路：三位受影响用户的真实读数均重新抽取出原来的正确“查看灯泡不改变其亮灭”规则。逐条回放原生合并确认：相同键和文本只刷新时间，不提高已有的负证据；规则仍被过滤。研究者只将这一条规则的证据恢复为正值，完整历史查询恢复，原生查询仍错；同时恢复两条灯泡规则后，两个视图在两次重复中均正确。普通学习组还从成功任务中学到了错误的“移动不改变电连接”规则，移除后这四次查询均正确。这些是同一检查点上的窄干预，不是攻击权限、任务成功率或通用防御；不同文本可创建新的正证据规则，不能称为不可逆。OEP 和 Belief Memory 已讨论错误经验的持续性与纠正困难，恢复失败本身不能作为新意证明。

规划器方向的新意约束：SWAAP 已通过修改少量微调转移的下一状态，破坏 TD-MPC2 与 DINO-WM 的规划，并包含多任务受害模型的初步迁移。因此“世界模型投毒使 MPC 失败”不能当作新的主张；仅行动权限也已见于 SCAB。后续需要真正新的学习机制及任务证据，而非替换执行器后重复既有结论。

原先的结构化例外规则方向也用官方 Pro 复查过，未学到有效的例外规则，停止在洁净门槛，见结果。当前 ScienceWorld 执行评测仍从官方可达中间状态继续，尚无该环境从重置开始的完整受害者基准，也没有达到会议级确认实验的扩量条件。

潜变量规划器的短测

已接入作者公开的 TD-MPC2 48M、三十任务检查点，保留原始三步、512 候选轨迹规划器。检查点文件哈希匹配，格式转换后的价值头与原始权重逐头计算相符，最大绝对差约 1.53×10⁻⁵。当前依赖版本及兼容处理见来源记录，不声称严格复现原论文软件环境。两个初态中，行走任务原生规划约 972／973 分，单策略约 395／182 分；机械臂到达任务规划为 983／991 分。猎豹奔跑与倒立摆洁净能力不足，行走机器人的跑步也有一次未过预设 600 分门槛，均不用于归因攻击损失，见补充洁净检查。

初步适配对照

到达任务的正常规划与随机动作各采集三百次真实交互；随后仅做四十步共享动力学更新，冻结其余部分。到达任务由 983 分分别变为 981／984，行走任务由 972.4 分分别变为 975.0／974.9，见短测结果。以向后行走为源、向前行走为目标，得分分别为 972.3／973.8，仍未退化，见共享物理任务结果。相同预算改用原始完整更新函数，目标得分为洁净 972.4、正常 954.8、随机 973.2，见完整更新结果。正常组单次小幅下降不能当作攻击效应。

定向搜索没有造成任务损害：在向后行走环境中，用两代搜索产生十六段、各三百次真实动作的课程；共用 4,800 次源环境参考交互，选中的部署课程仍只有三百次。每个候选独立从原检查点做四十次原始完整更新。按干净模型的候选规划排序损失选择后，目标向前行走两个保留初态的得分为 976.8／990.8，洁净为 973.1／989.7；正常、随机及最大预测误差基线也均未明显受损，见实际任务结果。代理排序变化没有转化成任务损失。

进一步允许攻击者运行目标参考模拟器，以完整五百步实际回报在同一十六候选中选择，新增 9,500 次目标参考交互。最差候选 927.9 分，比最低对照 954.8 只低 26.9 分，未达到预设“低于每个对照至少 100 分”的门槛，因此未运行最终确认初态，见选择结果。固定该候选、将学习增加到 120／300 次，另一初态得分为 966.0／957.0；普通学习为 972.0／965.6，洁净为 973.1，见更新次数短测。这些开发短测不足以支持稳定或较大的攻击损害，也没有进行显著性检验；停止沿本设置增加训练。

上述在线适配由本研究加在官方离线多任务检查点上，保留原始更新函数但使用 batch 32 和近期源任务数据，没有原训练回放库。三百次更新只在更新与新增动作的数量比例上接近原在线训练，不是原生训练协议复现。攻击可访问权重和参考模拟器，全部记录与奖励来自实际执行，没有改写；这些是开发短测，不是已有投毒算法的完整复现或新算法贡献。受攻击后的潜变量模型修复和集体任务损失尚无支持。

多步计划的直接执行检查

新增官方 Pro 的完整计划短测，检验真实经验是否会使模型把不兼容的局部结果拼成不可执行计划。模型逐步预测预先固定的候选计划，选择最短的预测可行计划，再在独立初态的真实程序环境中完整执行；沿用原生经验检索、规则抽取和证据更新的结构化状态适配。生产线重启和往返配送均通过洁净检查；三种课程各学习十六个真实动作后，洁净、普通、随机、定向四组均选对可行计划并执行成功，共八次成功，见计划与执行轨迹。定时通行在攻击前就把重新激活错误预测为不续期，未运行该任务的攻击课程。这是三个小型程序任务的筛选，不是原生 Agent 的自主规划基准，也不支持当前定向课程造成组合性损害。

WorldKernel 已讨论预测与整体可行性、反事实一致性的区别；一般性的“一步预测不足以保证完整推理”不能作为新意。对这里使用的确定性全状态环境，如果模型在所有递归访问的状态动作上都预测准确，多步轨迹必然准确。因此后续若出现效果，必须定位真实交互诱发的具体模型变化，不能把所有一步预测正确和错误多步轨迹同时作为未经限定的主张。

未知规则任务中的共享学习

已接通 DiscoveryWorld 官方环境，使用未改动的 Hypothesizer 行动、反思和知识压缩函数，从原始重置状态开始，最多一百个游戏步骤。反应堆任务要求根据已校准晶体的测量结果推断第三个晶体的频率；另一个植物任务要求找出未知的有效养分。目前均为 Easy、开发种子 2。

共享历史由十一条真实动作采集：只测量晶体 1、2 并读取其已校准反应堆频率，再经原生模块形成知识，花费十二次 API 请求。另一代理在同种子的新环境中读取未经修改的知识；晶体 3 的读数和答案没有传入。旧位置和库存记录也被保留，可能在重置后失效。动作、测量和真实任务评分见 学习依赖短测。共享组的执行成本与前期采集成本分别报告，不能把源课程开销当作零。

  • 反应堆／Pro 关闭推理／无历史：用满动作预算，任务未完成；已记录 100 步。运行状态
  • 反应堆／Pro 关闭推理／共享历史：API 余额不足中断，不计任务成败；已记录 91 步。运行状态
  • 反应堆／Pro 高推理／无历史：因接口与输出可靠性问题停止，不计任务成败；已记录 12 步。运行状态
  • 反应堆／Pro 高推理／共享历史：因接口与输出可靠性问题停止，不计任务成败；已记录 8 步。运行状态
  • 反应堆／Flash 高推理／接口说明修订／无历史：API 余额不足中断，不计任务成败；已记录 2 步。运行状态
  • 反应堆／Flash 高推理／接口说明修订／共享历史：API 余额不足中断，不计任务成败；已记录 5 步。运行状态
  • 反应堆／网关 glm-5.3-flash／接口说明修订／无历史：完成任务；已记录 33 步。运行状态
  • 反应堆／网关 glm-5.3-flash／接口说明修订／共享历史：完成任务；已记录 19 步。运行状态
  • 植物养分／Flash 高推理／无历史：因接口与输出可靠性问题停止，不计任务成败；已记录 18 步。运行状态
  • 植物养分／Flash 高推理／接口说明修订／无历史：API 余额不足中断，不计任务成败；已记录 0 步。运行状态
  • 植物养分／网关 glm-5.3／接口说明修订／无历史：完成任务；已记录 16 步。运行状态

关闭推理与开启高推理分别比较，均记录实际请求参数；Pro 高推理出现输出限额耗尽及截断，保留失败请求和原生解析失败。两条 Pro 高推理试运行提前停止，不算任务失败。新一轮 Flash 高推理同时为无历史与共享历史组补充同一句接口说明：列在 nearbyAgents 的设备可以用 TALK 打开控制对话。这来自公开接口，不提供频率或养分答案；原动作空间、反思与压缩仍相同。旧运行与新运行不用于直接估计单一因素效应。计算出频率不等于实际启动反应堆，局部分数也不等于任务完成。该阶段用于确定可用的任务与代理配置，不能作为攻击成功或新意证据。

官方 API 的 HTTP 402（Insufficient Balance）中断了部分运行，这些不能填成任务失败。主网关也出现间歇性超时；续跑重放原来已经返回的回复，并检查完整提示相等，保留先前的请求费用。续跑属于同一条轨迹，不能计作独立重复。

用户此前提供的主网关通过重新连通检查，已用 glm-5.3／glm-5.3-flash 别名另开独立任务运行，仍采用同一设备接口说明。后端身份未经独立核验，不能冒充官方模型复现；各组重新从初态开始，没有在旧运行中切换骨干。

植物养分无历史组在 16 步、41 次 API 请求后完成。反应堆无历史组在 33 步、累计 84 次请求后完成，共享历史组在 19 步、46 次请求后完成。共享组的前期采集另需 11 步、12 次请求，因此计入采集后是 30 步，对比无历史组的 33 步。请求数包括接口重试；无历史组两次续跑仍只是一条轨迹。成功来自环境的 completedSuccessfully 标志；这是单个开发世界中的结果，不能估计稳定收益。

十一动作的参考设备实验已完成，未支持“把可调设置误学为物理规律”的机制：调高 1000 Hz 后，最终压缩的知识仍保留原始正确测量对，并明确记下失去校准。调高 1 Hz 的对照保留了正确的 Channel 1 × 66 规律，但最后的压缩回复被截断，原生程序保留未压缩知识，因此不能声称完成了严格的压缩后对比。不再为这组课程运行完整受害任务。详见 源课程结果。

成功容差短测也已完成：在没有既有知识时，先对两个参考设备做同向或反向的 1 Hz 调整，再测量晶体。两组各 21 条动作全部成功，两个参考设备始终保持启动；分别花费 26、27 次请求。两组最终压缩均成功，且都保留原始校准值、明确区分手动调整，并得到正确的 Channel 1 × 66 规律。因此，研究者手算的错误外推没有在原生学习器中出现，不再扩展这条课程。详见 成功容差结果。

植物移栽实验已完成：两组各 21 条真实动作全部成功，原生知识整理均成功解析。对照组确认钛；移栽组丢失搬动来历，留下错误的“钍或钾”待验证判断。随后用两份原样知识运行完整任务，两组都提交钛并成功：对照组 3 步／6 次请求，移栽知识组 6 步／13 次请求（含一次输出截断重试）。后者第 4 步结合新观察中的蘑菇位置与已有的正确土壤测量，修正了结论。源阶段分别为 35、27 次请求，对照源因接口中断续跑三次，仍是一条轨迹。该课程有知识偏差，却没有造成任务失败；单次多出 3 步不足以证明稳定成本攻击，停止扩展。它也不是跨任务转移的证据。详见 植物移栽结果与任务对照。

Self-Confirming Superposition Traps 已研究策略选择数据、表征干扰与次优行为的自维持关系。因此一般性的“错误模型改变探索并自我维持”也需要进一步区分，不能仅靠换到科学发现任务支撑新意。

CausalGame 原生能力检查

使用原生 hybrid 执行器，固定 antenna_trap／种子 2／glm-5.3，保留 200 个探索样本、10 次部署上限及 75% 成功门槛。有效运行执行 9 次部署、用完 200 个样本，最终 703/1000（70.3%），未通过能力检查；11 次 API 请求全部成功。此前一轮因工作路径泄露实验名称而主动停止并排除，耗用 7 次请求（其中 1 次中断时尚无返回记录）；该轮主接口曾断连，备用接口恢复，已提醒用户。此处没有攻击实验，不支持任务损害或新机制主张，也不能从单次结果概括所有模型的能力。 详见 能力检查记录。

SafePred 候选经源码与论文检查后停止：被拦截动作确实会导出模型预测，但记录明确标为 prediction，写入独立的预测训练目录，没有进入这里检查的真实观测数据或默认经验回放。论文描述离线蒸馏，尚无原生在线自反馈链路证据。因此不能把“导出训练样本”当成已发生的学习污染；本次未运行模型、攻击或 GPU 实验。 详见 源码证据与检查范围。

只读规划查询候选完成短测：在研究者定义的共用单个 TD-MPC2 规划器服务中，每次受害者行动前插入一次反向行走历史观察的规划查询，共 500 次。正常回报 972.41，干扰后 973.11，没有下降；查询会改变热启动方案，但未造成任务损失。查询前后隔离随机数消耗，恢复受害者热启动方案后，全部观察、动作、奖励与正常轨迹逐项完全相同，世界模型与策略张量哈希也保持相同。三个完整 500 步回合的推理约 97 秒，训练和 API 调用均为零。这是单种子开发短测，不能外推鲁棒性，也不能称为原生多租户系统漏洞；停止扩展这条课程。 详见 规划查询短测。

2026-10-09，官方 Gemini API 当前可用。本轮严格机制搜索 HTTP200 且返回合法 JSON，但只用于候选生成，未作为实验或新意证据。此前一次隐藏接口搜索的应用层解析错误和一次提供商过滤仍保留在日志中。当前共记录29次官方 Gemini 请求、28次成功、1次提供商过滤。旧 GLM 主接口故障已提醒，Gemini 不跨提供商静默切换。 详见 接口更新记录。

反事实查询旧结果的统计与系统边界已于 2026-10-15 修正：原逐行留一把同 seed 的异标签配对留在训练，旧 0% 不能作为有效的独立验证。整对留出后，弱偏好和四组 geometry 结果均为 50%；纯潜变量目标组的查询／选中首动作／拼接分类分别为 23/24、19/24、21/24。后者并非条件互信息或增量泄漏证明。所用 dynamics 组件是原生预训练模型，但 CEM 目标为新增代理；v1/geometry 的奖励 logits 均值及跨轮累计还有实现错误，且动作未执行。因而当前没有原生隐私损害或普遍无泄漏结论。修正依据；完整重分析。

原生 MBRL-Lib CartPole 短测把每次想象的高斯残差改为跨时间完全持久，同时检查普通均值 CEM 和 alpha=0.2 的 CVaR-CEM。残差均值约0、标准差约1，相邻乘积约1，参数哈希未变；但均值规划平均回报为原生183、干预184，CVaR 两个种子均为200/200。均值规划一升一降、没有一致的有害效应；CVaR 未见回报下降，候选不升级。相关轨迹不确定性已有直接先行工作，不能把相关性本身作为新意。 详见 跨时间耦合短测、CVaR原始结果。

分歧压平在单任务出现局部损害：CVaR-CEM 两种子从200/200降至149/181，均值CEM从194/200降至146/199；两者均未满足预设的每个种子至少下降20%门槛。真实采样器在100条保留验证转移上保持平衡一步均值（最大误差2.98e-8），却改变预测分布；普通规划同样受损，未建立风险误判专属机制。阈值1.0对照仍有0.0809%想象行触发，得200，不能称为防御验证。保留局部任务损害，暂不接受高新意主张或扩大训练。 详见 成员分歧压平短测、一步采样验证、CVaR原始结果。

Gemini 已用于真实任务决策：保持现有 Qwen 世界模型与固定经验记忆，使用官方 gemini-3.8-flash 作为 ScienceWorld 执行 Agent。洁净／正常经验／攻击经验三臂均得100分，分别2／3／2步。攻击组仍收到错误“灯亮”预测，但真实查看灯泡后正确分类；本轮未复现测量替代导致的任务损失。14次官方API调用全部成功；这是一个物理实例的任务续接，不是多实例成功率。 详见 Gemini执行复核、三臂轨迹。

最新 Gemini 复核认为，在现有负面证据、直接先验和固定新意约束下，没有可直接升级的可信候选。提出的两个待筛选方向分别接近潜空间收缩/后门式动力学扰动和已有动作反射/不确定性通道；它们只是想法，不构成实验或新意证据。 不启动长时间 GPU。保留当前无最终主张状态；只有出现机制上独立、可执行且能避开既有 UQ/潜空间扰动先验的候选，才重新进入短门槛实验。 详见 最新候选复核记录、原始复核。

CartPole 的退化不能归因于非交换性：无符号动作变化对照同样退化；HalfCheetah 多维动作短测没有方向性损害。保留一步平价只说明操作边界，不支持新安全主张；不扩大训练或 GPU。 详见 动作顺序短测、CartPole 结果。

结构化的反向排序和循环路由没有造成短 CEM 损害，只有随机路由退化；因此不能把随机扰动的失败归因于动作反事实对应关系。暂不升级或扩大。 详见 动作反事实对应短测、原始结果。

终止位在候选动作组之间重分配，保持状态、奖励、模型权重和整批终止位多重集不变；两个预先固定种子的原生 CEM 均为50分，反向、循环和随机路由也均为50分。没有原生任务损害，退役该候选，不扩大训练。 详见 终止归属路由短测、种子810结果、种子811结果。

三种训练种子中，递归模型和知道真实观测规律的精确贝叶斯规划器都从100%降到0%；知道线索被反转的攻击感知贝叶斯规划器恢复到100%。局部转移元组支持完全保持，但完整历史多重集不保持。因此该短测证明的是有效观测替换／隐藏分支错配，而不是世界模型独有的安全机制，退役，不进入原生任务或 GPU 扩展。 详见 分支来源短测、原始结果。

低维 reacher 的合法补零经验没有选择性压低 walker 的未访问动作方向：MPC 源使高维动作敏感度变化 0.34%，低维动作变化 0.44%；随机源分别变化 1.85% 与 1.52%。walker 一步潜空间误差比值为 1.026 和 1.141，已有原生回报为 972.30 和 973.75（洁净 972.41）。因此该候选没有通过选择性不变量和任务损害门槛，退役，不扩大 GPU。 详见 补零动作短测、原始结果。

保留源任务优化器状态相对同一污染后权重的重置对照只下降0.80分（MPC源）或3.57分（随机源）；相对洁净受害者下降1.32和7.91分，均未达到100分门槛。退役该优化器状态候选，不扩大训练。 详见 优化器状态短测、原始结果。

高频合法动作没有造成规划器专属损害：dither 组 walker 回报 973.35，洁净 973.14，等能量白噪声 975.17；相对白噪声仅高频组低 1.82 分，未达到 100 分门槛。退役该候选，不运行确认回合。 详见 高频动作短测、原始结果。

定向 Gemini 机制搜索提出的五个方向分别落入已有的递归漂移、奖励梯度、潜空间塌缩、不确定性抑制或曲率扰动近邻；没有一个同时满足独立机制、原生规划接口和短门槛条件，因此不把模型意见当作主张或实验结果。详见 定向搜索原始结果。

本轮 Gemini 隐藏接口搜索返回了四个候选，但本地包装器把响应字典误传给字符串解析器；HTTP200 原始内容已保留，未作为证据。正交可达边界、曲率剪切和梯度反馈候选分别接近既有反事实/几何扰动或需要未授权梯度权限；跨任务可行性纠缠属于普通多任务负迁移，缺少动作授权的原生学习路径和任务门槛。因此不启动 GPU。详见 搜索记录。

最新严格 Gemini 机制筛选明确要求原生控制器、合法交互或授权推理操作、两分钟内的数字化证伪门槛，并排除一般投毒、观测替换和规划器改动；返回结果为没有候选。因此不把已有安全校准方法改写成新攻击，也不启动 GPU。详见 本轮筛选。

本轮候选复核尚未形成新机制证据。“真实经验压制探索”与已有主动学习攻击、不确定性投毒直接重叠，不接受其泛化新颖性主张。FedWorld 提供了正确规则因适用范围判断而停止共享的协议线索，但原论文已分析保护与迁移的权衡，且本轮未找到公开实现；单个例外也可能被客户端资格和分组机制隔离。因此暂不构造替代协议或启动新的能力测试。本轮没有付费 API 请求或 GPU 实验。 对“真实共享知识耗尽其他代理探索激励”的进一步源码检查也未通过：MACE 保留各代理自己的计数或 RND 新颖度；Plan2Explore 没有这里需要的独立贡献者共享协议，且原生训练会继续通过想象轨迹训练任务策略。不能通过新增共享机制或冻结任务策略来冒充原生结果。Gamma-World 的后续只读可行性复查被自动安全审核拒绝，提示可能涉及网络安全风险；该分支未完成，也没有实验结果。 详见 候选筛选与文献约束。

全局回报尺度短测完成，未支持任务损害：原始组973.14分，普通尺度训练组969.00分，高尺度训练组972.43分。两训练组从同一模型、相同批次和随机数状态开始，各做40次原生策略更新；起始尺度4.68与10.67取自此前真实经验日志，之后继续原生更新，末值4.08与8.08。策略参数确实改变且两组不同，全部72个非策略张量保持完全相同。三组各运行原始规划器500步，总耗时约95秒，零API请求。高尺度组相对原始组仅低0.71分、反而高于普通尺度组3.43分，未达到相对两种对照均下降100分的预设门槛，停止该设置。这是单个任务初态中的研究者尺度干预，不是完整贡献者攻击，也不证明其他设置安全。 详见 回报尺度短测。

机制与正向方法仍未建立足够新颖性。功能保持的学习几何调制、纠错停止核、经验晋升为共享技能，均找到对应已有操作。前轮在原样 OPINE 学习代码中复现了查询改变隐藏记忆；本轮确认上游提示已要求规划分支的连续性保护，且原生规划短测未触发该现象，因此停止把它作为新概念扩展。新核对的原始论文还明确覆盖未来可纠正性、学习获取证据的能力，以及机制记忆的选择性更新；这些泛化表述不能直接计为我们的贡献。后续独立方法评议的主、备用请求均超时，无回复。本轮没有新增环境实验或GPU工作，尚无新方法优势可支持扩大实验。 详见 候选核实记录。

DMO网络必要条件短测已完成（seed31；真实DMControl数据；1000次初始NLL更新；模型计算5.95秒，采集2.78秒，0次API）。干净模型测试导数与真实有限差分的余弦中位数0.998。预先固定的4个末层扰动均未通过预测误差≤1.1倍门槛：lambda=1可翻转相对干净模型的dev导数（余弦−0.997），但dev预测MSE为769.7倍；lambda=1000的MSE最低，为1.226倍，导数方向基本不变。没有候选进入旋转对照或128次再训练，因此不能判断持续性，也不能声称正常学习清除了扰动。本结果仅否定该固定构造通过既定门槛，不否定一般机制；无actor或任务损害证据，不扩大此设置。 详见 导数扰动短测。

修正迁移的必要条件短测已完成：在给定正确仿射函数形式、但未知传感器绑定和系数的条件下，三个原生 Reactor Lab 实例的六个新晶体均无候选共同支持的设定值；排除超出设备范围的候选后仍相差约2698–6164 Hz。相同候选空间下，该共识操作与常规候选模型推断等价。更强的简单基线仅按系数表达长度选择规则，实际完成3/3任务并激活6/6待调反应堆，未使用源任务经验。两项完成的CPU短测分别约7.4/8.4秒，无API或GPU。结果只支持停止推进这版共识方案，不证明一般修正迁移无效；基线给定了正确函数族且采用脚本导航，不能计为新方法或完整LLM代理结果。 详见 原生任务基线结果与修正迁移短测。

公开 OPINE 轨迹及学习代码中出现了具体的查询副作用：25个游戏、792个代码版本、12340条有效转移中有52组完整帧和动作相同但后继不同的记录；50组差异仅涉及已忽略的计数条，1组差异在场景内，另1组缺少当时的掩码。对 dc22 的两个原样学习程序按真实第六关历史初始化后，分别正确回放112/112和825/825条转移；同一输入多查询一次，使其中1条和9条从正确变错，恢复查询前的模块记忆后均恢复。每次清空记忆则只能预测对109/112和813/825条，说明简单禁用记忆也会丢失已有能力。无效点击对照没有影响。12次有深度限制的原生规划检查均未触发记忆或预测改变：该规划器遗漏了关键传送动作，也没有找到计划。全部为一个游戏的样本内代码诊断，约18.7秒CPU，无API/GPU；不能计作攻击、原生任务损失或新方法优势。 后续源码核对发现，上游提示词已明确允许累积隐藏状态并要求规划分支的连续性保护；当前复现是已有防护未落实的局部现象，因此停止把它作为新概念扩展。此提示词核对针对固定仓库版本，不声称已还原每个历史轮次的提示。 详见 查询与模型记忆诊断及运行记录。

新增的状态同步基线检查停在原生重放兼容性门槛：DC22 前482个动作与作者存档逐像素一致，第483个动作（索引482）出现6个场景像素差异。第二次从初态重放复现差异，该动作只有一个动画帧，未发现可替代的匹配中间帧；原因仍未查明。尚未到达计划比较的第762/766步，因此同步搜索和原生后续探针均未运行，不能记为方法成功或失败。通过行动降低对历史的依赖已有 homing/reset 策略先例；新学习操作和任务收益仍未建立。本项未使用模型API或GPU。 详见 状态同步基线检查及重放记录。

最终实验目标

目标仍为ICLR会议论文级证据：形成一个广泛、清晰的世界模型安全主张，在多类核心任务上用强基线、多个可核验模型与决定性的机制干预验证安全影响。允许依据反证修改安全机制；不能把预测变化、某个实现缺陷、一般学习能力提升或一个任务中的基线改进当作最终贡献。只有短测支持最终机制后才扩大实验；目前新意7–7.5目标和确认性规模均未达成。

复现入口

PYTHONPATH=src python -m unittest discover -s tests -v

PYTHONPATH=src python scripts/summarize.py

实验入口位于 scripts/pilot_*.py；机器可读结果与用量；研究路线与扩量条件。API 凭据存放在项目目录之外，未写入文档和调用日志。第三方网关别名仍不视作已核验后端；本地权重版本与新增 DeepSeek 官方端点分别留有记录。

关键文献与区别

  • Self-Evolving World Models for LLM Agent Planning：Native code implements action-Jaccard retrieval, preservation-rule extraction, global displayed-rule credit, positive-evidence rendering. Code is pinned in roadmap.
  • OEP: Poisoning Self-Evolving LLM Agents via Locally Correct but Non-Transferable Experiences：Locally correct examples plus attacker-authored hypothetical consequences induce harmful overgeneralization. Generic truthful-experience poisoning is already covered; action-generated evidence and subsequent learning dynamics need separate contributions.
  • When Experience Becomes Instruction: Trajectory Poisoning in Self-Evolving Agent Skill Systems：PoisonedEvolution studies evidence promotion and attribution from contributed trajectories into skills. Inspect full protocol before implementing comparator.
  • HiMPO: Hindsight-Informed Memory Policy Optimization for Less-Entangled Credit in Long-Horizon Agents：Blame leakage in memory optimization is known; showing it exists is insufficient novelty. Adversarial action selection, cross-agent transfer, and learning feedback would need new evidence.
  • Belief Memory: Agent Memory Under Partial Observability：Discusses agents committing to beliefs, selecting actions that avoid alternatives, and reinforcing error. Broad self-confirmation alone is not new.
  • Memory Injection Attacks on LLM Agents via Query-Only Interaction：Query-based memory injection uses crafted text; different privileges from action-only simulator interaction.
  • FedWorld: Scope-Aware Federation of Agent World Models：Addresses differing client dynamics and rule scope. Same-dynamics adversarial effects would be distinct.
  • Aligning Agentic World Models via Knowledgeable Experience Learning：Selected official plugin prompts downloaded and pinned. Structured process-learning adapter uses reflection prompts verbatim, exact state comparison, and all-rule reading for small pilots; not a native benchmark reproduction.
  • Policy Teaching via Environment Poisoning: Training-time Adversarial Attacks against Reinforcement Learning：Manipulates rewards or transition dynamics to teach a target policy; the proposed action-only setting must keep those dynamics and feedback unchanged. Generic adversarial teaching is not a novelty claim.
  • Fox in the Henhouse: Supply-Chain Backdoor Attacks Against Reinforcement Learning：SCAB uses only legitimate actions of an externally supplied agent, without editing victim observations, rewards, or policy. Thus action-only poisoning itself is not new. Its backdoor is trained into an interacting victim policy through implicit rewards; shared frozen-world-model memory and noninteracting later victims would require distinct evidence.
  • Causally Correct Partial Models for Reinforcement Learning：Primary FuzzyBear example and equations1–2 inspected: a future behavior action provides evidence about an intermediate random state; treating this observational action sequence as intervention can produce harmful planning even with correct fitted conditionals. Generic future-action confounding is therefore already prior art; no reproduction.
  • Learning Implicit Causal World Models from Multi-Agent Demonstrations：The preprint explicitly separates physical dynamics from strategic-agent correlations and studies full and partial observation. A new attack claim must establish more than generic policy-induced confounding.
  • What Eviction Destroys: A Restore-Counterfactual Audit of Forgetting in Agent Memory：Separates stored-evidence eviction from retrieval failure using restored-evidence counterfactuals. Generic restore interventions and recoverable-versus-irreversible memory error are not new; the current experiment concerns retained corrective experience excluded by native retrieval.
  • MemTX: Transactional Belief Commit for Stateful Agent Memory：Stages shared belief writes and supports dependent-record repair. Shared-memory propagation and recovery alone are insufficient novelty; this project still needs action-only induced model changes and actual downstream effects.
  • From Shield to Target: Denial-of-Service Attacks on LLM-Based Agent Guardrails：Crafted text drives guardrail reasoning amplification and shared-infrastructure starvation. Generic computational amplification or cross-agent cost externalities are not new. The current isolated empty-output cases do not establish a new DoS attack.
  • Attacking the Trusted Imagination: Oracle-Level Integrity Attacks on Imagine-then-Act World Models：已研究通过有界观察扰动破坏想象结果，并检验想象驱动规划中的攻击特异任务失败。仅把预测错误升级为决策错误并不自动构成新颖主张。
  • TrojanWorld: Backdooring World-Model Agents via Imagination Steering：Primary sections3–4 inspected. Physical observation trigger, frozen decision module feedback, clean behavior anchoring, and observation-conditioned post-removal propagation. Recurrent hidden corruption and constrained decision-facing model editing alone do not establish novelty.
  • MemoryGraft: Persistent Compromise of LLM Agents via Poisoned Experience Retrieval：通过可摄入文件植入伪造成功经验，量化少量恶意记录在检索中的高占比，并讨论跳过验证等行为风险。主要实证指标是检索污染率，不能等同于已测得的任务失败率。本项目的真实动作权限不同，但经验检索偏置和跨会话持续影响已有明确先例。
  • Stealthy World Model Manipulation via Data Poisoning：SWAAP changes a bounded fraction of recorded next-state targets during fine-tuning; evaluates downstream MPC harm on TD-MPC2, with DINO-WM extension and preliminary single-task-surrogate to multitask-victim transfer. Therefore planner corruption and cross-task transfer alone are not new. Its data-editing permission differs from genuine action-generated learning, which still needs a separate substantive contribution.
  • BrainWash: A Poisoning Attack to Forget in Continual Learning：Induces forgetting of earlier tasks by poisoning later-task data, without earlier-task data access. Therefore cross-task forgetting itself is not a new security contribution. Primary abstract checked; method implementation not yet reproduced.
  • Targeting World Models to Compromise Robot Learning Pipelines：Perturbed teleoperation video frames induce harmful synthetic trajectories and downstream policy backdoors. Thus propagation through imagined data is already studied. The attacker edits frames with white-box model access, unlike the current genuine-action search.
  • WorldKernel: A World Model is the Coupling Kernel of Admissible Possible Worlds：Distinguishes predictive information from admissibility and unidentified counterfactual coupling; also studies injected joint-only failure detection. It is not evidence of an action-only shared-model learning attack. Generic claims about local prediction versus global consistency need a distinct contribution.
  • Self-Confirming Superposition Traps in Reinforcement Learning：Shows that policy-selected co-activation can sustain suboptimal control even under optimal representation fitting. Protected fitting/replay improves some public tasks, including exploratory DreamerV3-Crafter results. Generic self-confirming world-model feedback and preserving neglected experience are not by themselves a new contribution; this is not a demonstrated action-only malicious contributor attack.
  • The Corroboration Illusion: When More News Makes LLM Forecasts Less True：LLM-written news inserted into a retrieval corpus can shift probabilistic forecasts; the paper evaluates placebo controls, retrieval filtering and aggregation defenses. More sources or corroborating records alone do not establish a new collective-learning mechanism. This method permits fabricated natural-language evidence, unlike our real simulator actions.
  • Fresh Memory, Stale Plans: Dependency-Scoped Validation for Distributed LLM-Agent Memory：Studies stale-plan execution after shared records change and validates exact plan dependencies before protected actions. Generic temporal inconsistency or stale plans in multi-agent memory are already studied; a new experiment must demonstrate a distinct learning mechanism.
  • MAPLE-Guard: Memory-Aware Link Enforcement Against Memory-Link Poisoning in Multi-Agent Systems：Studies poisoned experiences written through dialogue or direct memory, then retrieved, promoted and reused across agents. Memory-link persistence and harm to agents without direct attacker communication are not new by themselves. Its attacker-authored text permissions differ from our simulator-generated action feedback.
  • Mind the Execution Gap: Action-Semantic Mismatch in World-Model Control：Studies timeline mismatch in TD-MPC2 and attribution to unapplied commands in DreamerV3, with execution-consistent interfaces. Asynchronous action misattribution alone cannot be treated as a new shared-world-model mechanism.
  • Securing LLM-Agent Long-Term Memory Against Poisoning: Non-Malleable, Origin-Bound Authority with Machine-Checked Guarantees：Origin-label propagation and independent corroboration gate consequential actions. Generic origin laundering through memory compression is already studied. This paper labels origin by authenticated input channel; truthful observations of physically intervened world states require a separate causal interpretation, which its channel-origin assumptions do not establish. That distinction alone is not a novelty claim.
  • causal-memory: causal memory layer for AI agents：Stores typed decision–outcome edges outside the context window to survive compaction. Therefore generic loss of causal history under memory summarization, or adding an external causal table, is not a new mechanism by itself.
  • False Prophets: On the Security of World Models in Agentic Systems：Studies approximate terminal simulation failures from computation, randomness, missing configuration, external state and token limits, among other channels. Evaluates exact exit-code and output prediction for700scripts on Qwen-AgentWorld35B and CWM32B. This forecast benchmark does not make a prediction mismatch equivalent to a measured downstream agent failure. Generic simulation-induced control-flow harm is already covered.
  • CausalGame: Benchmarking Causal Thinking of LLM Agents in Games：Agents collect observations under a limited experiment budget, then submit one final design for separate evaluation. Native variants already include selection bias, hidden confounding, no history, no selection bias and distribution shift. Merely showing an agent follow biased historical evidence would not establish a new mechanism. Published per-scenario results include successes; aggregate performance should not be read as every model failing every individual scenario.
  • Verification Trap: Understanding Test-Time Selection Failures under False Premises in Code Generation：Studies generators and verifiers coupled by a false task premise, causing tests to miss counterexamples and selectors to choose incorrect programs despite correct candidates. Generic shared-belief verification blindness cannot be claimed new on that description alone.
  • SafePred: A Predictive Guardrail for Computer-Using Agents via World Models：The inspected implementation exports rejected-action predictions as separately labeled prediction training data, without adding them to ordinary experience replay. The paper describes offline distillation. Neither establishes the proposed online self-poisoning loop.
  • The World Model Remembers, the Actor Forgets: Dream Rehearsal for Continual Model-Based RL：In a continual MiniGrid setting, studies retained world-model knowledge alongside actor failure and separates frozen-head representation drift from failures under jointly trained heads. Generic representation drift or a capable predictor with a failing actor is already demonstrated; this does not establish an action-only security attack.
  • Truth Serum: Poisoning Machine Learning Models to Reveal Their Secrets：A malicious training-data contributor can enhance inference about other training examples. Merely combining poisoning and membership inference is therefore not a new research mechanism.
  • MRMMIA: Membership Inference Attacks on Memory in Chat Agents：Studies black-box output-based recall probes and richer-access attacks on chat-agent memory. Generic detection of remembered user facts is already studied; this work does not by itself settle an action-induced shared-world-model attack.
  • When the World Lies: Backdoor Attacks on Latent World Models for Downstream Control：Describes modifying a latent world-model checkpoint so that downstream clean policy optimization acquires attacker-desired triggered behavior. Generic latent-model backdoors and downstream policy harm are therefore not new; checkpoint access differs from action-only interaction.
  • Adversarial Active Learning：AISec 2014 explicitly studies correctly labelled attacker inputs manipulating query selection, wasting limited oracle effort, or avoiding inclusion. Section 4.1 provides an adaptive example. Generic suppression of exploration or acquisition through true evidence is therefore not a sufficient novelty claim.
  • Selection-Based Vulnerabilities: Clean-Label Backdoor Attacks in Active Learning：ALA optimizes clean-label poisoned inputs for uncertainty so acquisition functions select them. This constrains claims that targeting information acquisition rather than prediction accuracy is itself new; its backdoor objective is not identical to suppressed discovery.
  • Manipulating Out-Domain Uncertainty Estimation in Deep Neural Networks via Targeted Clean-Label Poisoning：CIKM 2023 targets out-of-domain uncertainty with clean-label training data, creating high confidence around a target while preserving overall utility. Generic truthful-data confidence manipulation requires distinction from this prior work.
  • FedWorld: Scope-Aware Federation of Agent World Models：M3 infers shared, cluster, private or unresolved rule scopes; M4 admits target-compatible rules only for local gaps. The paper explicitly models the cost of withholding correct rules. Authentic-exception denial remains a hypothesis: eligibility, clustering and local-first behavior may prevent it. No public implementation found in the inspected paper and searches.
  • MACE official implementation：The inspected runner computes agent-specific count or RND novelty before exchanging novelty scalars; the reward retains own novelty. No shared dynamics world model supplies the proposed depletion path. Cross-agent reward coupling alone is a different mechanism.
  • DreamerV2 official implementation with Plan2Explore：A single Agent owns world model, exploration actor and task actor. Native training trains the task actor through imagination after updating the model. Independent contributors sharing learned dynamics would require an added protocol; freezing normal actor adaptation would not establish a native effect.
  • Hail to the Thief: Exploring Attacks and Defenses in Decentralised GRPO：Correctly rewarded completions can poison decentralized GRPO, including long but correct generated answers. Appendix B.1-B.2 analyzes how poisoned-sample fractions alter normalized advantages. This constrains broad claims about truthful rewards or normalization as a new attack surface. The inspected mechanism uses completion-content gradients rather than establishing cross-task harm mediated only by persistent return-scale state.
  • Implicit Poisoning Attacks in Two-Agent Reinforcement Learning：A peer policy can poison another learner without changing underlying environment primitives; the formulation includes training from offline collected experience. Combined with SCAB deliberately rewarding target victim actions by losing games, this undermines a broad claim that truthful help can induce false beliefs about autonomous control.
  • Stored in Optimizer State, Valued by Later Training: A Causal Account of Subliminal Trait Transfer：First-moment-only transplants preserve current parameters and outputs, while later source-free updates produce differences. The same source-induced trainer state can have different behavioral effects under different continuations. This directly preempts optimizer-memory carryover as a new general mechanism. Identical-parameter states are diagnostic interventions; the paper does not establish our proposed genuine-environment-action attack.
  • Fooling Neural Network Interpretations via Adversarial Model Manipulation：NeurIPS2019 explanation manipulation preempts output-versus-gradient separation as a new mathematical mechanism. It does not by itself establish downstream policy-learning harm under real-forward DMO and ordinary corrective updates.
  • Gradient-based Analysis of NLP Models is Manipulable：Facade models dominate input gradients with little change to predictions. A derivative-provider attack needs an additional contribution beyond applying this observation to another consumer.
  • First Order Model-Based RL through Decoupled Backpropagation：DMO uses real simulator transitions and learned-model derivatives during policy training. Public StochSSM and Gaussian NLL updates can be tested independently; full native control requires a separate clean trained checkpoint and environment.
  • Control-Oriented Meta-Learning：Jointly meta-learns model features and adaptation/control parameters through closed-loop tracking loss. General augmented-state RL expressibility alone is not a novelty objection; our proposed repair-equivalence lacked an additional executable method.
  • VERDI: Retrieval Is Not Transfer for Continual World Model Optimization：Retrieves optimization hypotheses, validates locally with paired experiments, and refines diagnostic probes after contradicted transfer. Sharing procedures and locally rechecking outcomes alone is not a new principle.
  • Distribution-Specific Curvature Control with Finite-Sample Guarantees for Open-Weight Safety：HarmAlign applies spectral deformation selectively using activation subspaces while preserving the initial function. Generic reparameterization-based trainability control is not a new candidate.
  • Learning Abstract World Models for Value-preserving Planning with Options：Uses temporally extended actions and abstract models for value-preserving planning. A fixed feedback-correction protocol with cumulative costs and terminal-state law is an option-model instance.
  • Policy-Aware Model Learning for Policy Gradient Methods：Model-learning losses account for policy-gradient planning; matching how a model drives a policy learner is not by itself a new principle.
  • Understanding Gradient Clipping in Private SGD: A Geometric Perspective：Analyzes bias and convergence failure from asymmetric gradient distributions under clipping. Applying clipping to a random batch gradient does not remove the general bias mechanism.
  • PatchWorld: Gradient-Free Optimization of Executable World Models：Induces executable belief-state programs from offline trajectories and accepts repairs using replay validation. The paper explicitly does not evaluate cross-task or cross-domain transfer. Our Reactor Lab coefficient-search baseline is an independent diagnostic, not a reproduction of PatchWorld.
  • OPINE-World: Programmatic World Modeling with Ontology-error-Prioritized Interactive Exploration for ARC-AGI-3：Released dc22 programs naturally contain mutable hidden portal memory. Our exact observed-frame replay of two unchanged program versions fits the tested level-six prefixes; duplicate hypothetical calls can change subsequent predictions. This is our in-sample diagnostic, not a paper claim or a task-performance reproduction.
  • Agentic Safety is an Epistemic Property, Not a Behavioral One：Defines teachability of the evolving learner; discusses changes to hypothesis classes, update sensitivity, and edit acceptance. It proposes protected plasticity and bounded corrective interventions. Generic preservation of future correction is not a new contribution here.
  • Wide Learning: Learning to Reach Evidence：Defines learner-relative effective epistemic reach under fixed primitive affordances and bounded resources. Generic learning of informative experiment construction cannot serve as our novelty claim.
  • Causal Retention in Interactive Agents: Interface Factorization and Selective Adaptation：Defines a mechanism-probe interface and evidence-gated updates over action, context, target, value, and delay. Uses selective retesting to update changed mechanisms.
  • Reinforcement Learning in POMDPs Without Resets：Section 3 defines an action-based approximate reset strategy that contracts dependence on starting beliefs. Generic action-based removal of history dependence is therefore not a new concept.
  • Model Discovery Agent: LLM-assisted Bayesian experiment design for data-efficient discovery of mechanistic world models：MDA jointly expands mechanistic models and selects experiments; its appendix also selects observation likelihood/summary models by discrimination per compute. Merely adding observation-model selection is not a new contribution.
  • Causal Curiosity: RL Agents Discovering Self-supervised Experiments for Causal Representation Learning：Learns experimental action sequences that reveal causal factors and supports downstream transfer. Generic learning-to-measure is not a new concept; our finite template selector is not a reproduction of this method.
  • GalilAI: Out-of-Task Distribution Detection using Causal Active Experimentation for Safe Transfer RL：Section 2.3, equation 7 describes selecting actions by model and conditional-observation description length to isolate a causal factor. Law simplicity as an experimental selection criterion needs a distinct operation beyond this existing principle.
  • Learning programs by learning from failures：Generate-test-constrain and generalisation/specialisation pruning are prior operations.
  • Learning logic programs by explaining their failures：Failing subprograms refine the hypothesis space beyond rejecting the whole program. Our numeric finite-model filter is not a reproduction of Hempel.
  • DR. WELL: Dynamic Reasoning and Learning with Symbolic World Model for Embodied LLM-Based Multi-Agent Collaboration：Native code records per-plan task outcomes, preserves task commitments, and retrieves prototypes plus detailed plan instances. Our fixed-plan CUBE screen uses these actual components; it is not a full LLM negotiation benchmark.
  • Value-Decomposition Networks For Cooperative Multi-Agent Learning：The primary abstract identifies spurious team rewards and lazy agents and proposes agent-wise value decomposition. Generic credit assigned to non-contributing teammates is not a new concept. We did not reproduce VDN.
  • Baba in Wonderland: Online Self-Supervised Dynamics Discovery for Executable World Models：Alice already turns target-solving but regressing rejected programs into monotone experience-class splits, then uses those classes for preservation witnesses and frontier exploration. Rejection changing future learning is intended prior behavior, not our novel contribution. Paper and pinned native implementation inspected; public program evaluations are author checkpoints, not our trained replicas.
  • SkillOpt: Executive Strategy for Self-Evolving Agent Skills：Sections 3.5 and appendix describe an epoch-local rejected-edit buffer and optimizer-side meta memory. Candidate skills require strict selection-score improvement, while rejected edits and score drops condition later reflection. Generic learning from rejected candidates and validation-gated updates are existing contributions. No security effect established here.
  • OneWorld: Learning Consistent Physics Across Actions in World Models：Read primary method: a learned mechanism interpreter and a shared-world compatibility integral tie action-conditioned samples to one latent physical mechanism. Broad cross-action physical consistency is already addressed. No reproduction; source code was advertised for future release.
  • Causal Inference Through the Structural Causal Marginal Problem：Primary abstract inspected: synthesizing constraints from separate causal margins is existing work. No claim to a reproduced implementation or full theorem audit.
  • Joint Causal Inference from Multiple Contexts：Primary publication inspected: learning across contexts with possibly unknown intervention targets/types is existing work; not a new research claim here.
  • Synthesizing world models for bilevel planning：Primary abstract inspected: TheoryCoder grounds high-level abstractions in synthesized Python transition models and performs bilevel planning. Executable rules and rule-changing Baba tasks do not establish novelty by themselves. No reproduction.
  • StateTune: Transforming LLM-Assisted EDA Flow Tuning into a Stateful, Closed-Loop Process：Primary sections 3.2 and 5.4 inspected. The reported memory-poisoning ablation admits all emitted rules without validation; it is not an adversarial rejected-update-only experiment. It establishes that optimizer-memory quality and its joint effect on proposal and budget allocation are existing concerns.
  • Learning to Trust Experience: A Monitor-Trust-Regulator Framework for Learning under Unobservable Feedback Reliability：The primary abstract reports accuracy recovery with persistent belief lock-in under misleading feedback and proposes endogenous trust modulation. This limits novelty of the generic accuracy-versus-learning-state distinction, but does not establish our truthful-action or rejected-update mechanism.
  • Understanding Trajectory Heterogeneity in Federated World Model Learning：The October 2 preprint studies how client ownership and participation limit locally complete temporal windows in federated clinical world-model learning. Generic loss of long-window evidence under client partitioning is already studied; no adversarial action-only effect or new learning operator is established by our review.
  • DiscoverPhysics: Benchmarking LLMs for Out-of-the-Box Scientific Thinking：Provides simulated physical worlds, executable experiment interfaces, trajectory prediction scoring and a separate explanation assessment. We pinned the official repository and used only two deterministic native fixtures for a scalar-contrast operation screen; no full discovery-agent or official benchmark result is claimed.
  • Learning to Outgrow a Theory: Experimental Discovery Beyond the Initial Hypothesis Space：Jointly learns a structural edit and a diagnostic experiment, tests an entire current class using one shared parameterization across experiments, and uses sequential evidence for revision. The grammar includes observation-map changes. Generic edit-and-experiment pairing or transfer of such pairings cannot be claimed as new here.
  • EvoSCM: Scientific Belief Revision Through Causal Model Evolution and Experimentation：Maintains competing structural causal models, selects discriminating interventions, converts prediction discrepancies into model revisions, and studies transfer of evolved SCMs across model backbones on DiscoverPhysics. This is direct prior for generic causal-model evolution and sharing; it does not by itself establish equivalence to every possible experimental-program compiler.
  • Compiling Learning Problems into Adaptation Programs for Language Models：Learns to predict multiple behavioral consequences of candidate adaptation programs from pre-adaptation information and selects a program under a chosen utility. Its released code concerns LoRA adaptation programs; this is prior against claiming that learning how to choose updates or reusing predicted adaptation outcomes is itself new, not evidence that every experiment-construction algorithm is equivalent.
  • LLM-ACES: Closed-Loop Discovery of Dynamical Systems with LLM-Guided Adaptive Search：Combines LLM-proposed symbolic operator priors, candidate equation fitting and acquisition based on predictive disagreement. Its official project links public code and evaluates ODEBench and ODEBase; generic joint hypothesis-space search and active acquisition are already covered. This turn inspected the primary project and repository, not a reproduction.
  • Alchemy ideal observer：Native search explicitly recurses through deterministic state-changing actions before later information gains and offers an objective minimizing remaining world states.
  • Learning Game Strategies by Experimentation：DEL learns prerequisites and contextual conditions for experiments, postpones ambiguous tests, and lifts traces into reusable strategic plans. This is a direct prior against generic experiment-preparation novelty.
  • Deep Adaptive Design: Amortizing Sequential Bayesian Experimental Design：DAD learns an adaptive design policy from simulated histories using contrastive information objectives, amortizing sequential experimental design. Generic learning-to-select-experiments or faster deployment is therefore insufficient as the contribution. Primary abstract inspected; no local reproduction.
  • Passive learning of active causal strategies in agents and language models：The primary research page reports that passive training can teach strategies for causal intervention and exploitation at test time, including a setting with high-dimensional observations and language explanations. Merely learning experimental behavior from source traces is not a new broad claim. No reproduction.
  • On the Sensory Commutativity of Action Sequences for Embodied Agents：Studies sensory commutation of action sequences, defines SCP, and demonstrates environment learning and reinforcement-learning sample-efficiency uses. Commutation itself is not new.
  • QTRAN: Extending Metamorphic-Oracle Based Logical Bug Detection Techniques for Multiple-DBMS Dialect Support：Official code describes transfer and mutation of paired metamorphic tests across DBMS dialects. Generic cross-system transfer of relational tests is already studied. This turn inspected the official repository, not a reproduction.
  • Towards Automated Metamorphic Test Identification for Ocean System Models：Proposes learning physical-model metamorphic relations with a cost penalizing trivial identity and redundancy. Primary abstract inspected; no reproduction. Automated relation learning alone cannot be the claimed novelty.
  • Learning by Experimentation: Incremental Refinement of Incomplete Planning Domains：EXPO refines incomplete action knowledge after unexpected observations and performs additional experiments when needed. Generic failure-triggered context or precondition refinement is an existing operation.
  • World Models as Group Actions：Enforces identity, inverse and composition consistency for action-conditioned world models. Algebraic action consistency alone is not a new contribution.
  • TacticToe: Learning to Prove with Tactics：Learns tactic selection from earlier proofs and uses it to guide Monte Carlo tree search. Generic transfer of a proof-tactic policy is established; this does not itself refute a distinct experimental-design combination.
  • PROSE Framework: Usage and Version Spaces：Official documentation describes explicit, union and join representations for sets of programs consistent with example constraints. Preserving source-consistent program variants is an established synthesis operation.
  • Relational Program Synthesis：Synthesizes programs satisfying relational specifications through counterexample-guided synthesis and relational version-space learning. A relational continuation objective alone is not novel.
  • The Geometry of Sequential Learning: Lie-Bracket Prediction of Transfer Order：Studies gradient-update Lie brackets for pairwise transfer ordering and many-domain curriculum construction. Directly covers a generic commutator-based learning-order proposal. Reported performance was not independently reproduced.
  • Hierarchical Experimentalist Agents：Primary method section and experimental setup read. HExA already distills successes, failures, and partial skills into an evolving bank and transfers source skills to unseen Interphyre levels. Generic experimentation skill accumulation/transfer is not a new contribution. Our literal placement controls are not a reproduction of its actor-evolver loop.
  • FlowEvo: Self-Evolving Agents through the Co-Evolution of Workflows and Executable Skills：Official repo inspected at 36e81efd4d1fdbabca7366200b15808ebef157fa. Generic code path extracts passing programs/helpers and supports skills as execution or planning priors. ALFWorld template compiler uses seven supplied task blueprints; workflow meta-updates and demotion/promotion already exist. These observations scope a comparator and are not task-performance results.
  • Symbolon: Symbolic Execution by Learning Code Transformation：Primary abstract read: learns source-code transformations offline on small programs and transfers context-sensitive instantiations via a reusable skill library. Merely proposing reusable program edits is not sufficient novelty. No reproduction.
  • Causal-PIK: Causality-based Physical Reasoning with a Physics-Informed Kernel：Primary full method read: a learned short-horizon dynamics model estimates motion after first dynamic contact; causal-effect similarities define a GP kernel, and Bayesian optimization plus model-based candidate simulation selects actions. Near-goal trajectory progress is already used. Generic causal effect features or failure-driven continuous search are not new. No official implementation was located in the bounded project-page/search check; no full reproduction is claimed.
  • Model Discovery Agent: LLM-assisted Bayesian experiment design for data-efficient discovery of mechanistic world models：Primary methods and algorithms 1–3 inspected. LLM structural proposals are combined with Bayesian parameter/structure inference and value-of-information experiment design; predictive-check failures trigger hypothesis expansion. Generic failure-triggered new models plus informative experiments are prior operations. No reproduction here.
  • Agent Priors-guided Policy Learning：Primary sections 3–4 inspected. An agent segments demonstrations, implements alternative representation/action/loss priors for skill policies, and exposes each prior through a runtime composition interface. Generic model-designed structural priors and their reuse are existing work. This does not establish exact equivalence to an as-yet distinct experiment-construction rule; no reproduction.
  • Model metamers reveal divergent invariances between biological and artificial neural networks：Primary abstract, general procedure, optimization and relation-to-prior-work passages inspected via the indexed publisher article. Input optimization matches internal activations with fixed model weights; human responses test the inferred invariances. Generic model-equivalent stimulus construction and empirical mismatch are existing operations. This is a boundary for a contemplated state-equivalence experiment idea, not an implemented candidate or a claim that every dynamic extension is equivalent.
  • The iso-response method: measuring neuronal stimulus integration with closed-loop experiments：Authors’ method exposition read, especially iso-response geometry and closed-loop stimulus search. Varying stimulus parameters while keeping neural output constant isolates integration nonlinearities; adaptive root-finding traces response manifolds. Generic constant-response stimulus search is established prior, not a newly learned experimental operation.
  • Variance Reduction in Monte-Carlo Tree Search：Primary sections 2–3, especially common random numbers, read. Sharing simulated chance events across action branches changes variance of value differences without biasing marginal estimates; poor correlation can worsen it. Generic sampling-dependence effects are established. Our explicit adversarial phase construction needs a security distinction and learned-planner evidence, not a claim that correlation itself is new.
  • MBRL-Lib official implementation：MIT source pinned at 3f93cccfc8d635f74e335a2f07aab6e9a48fc021. Native GaussianMLP/ModelEnv/CEM used locally. Rank coupling reduced return with a smaller best-candidate planner, but upstream-default elite-mean CEM scored 200 in both tested conditions. This is a local sampler intervention, not a discovered deployment vulnerability.
  • Using Common Random Numbers for Simulation-based Planning with Rollouts：Primary sections 1–2 and conclusion read. Shared simulator randomness can increase policy-value-difference variance; depth-dependent sharing provides a variance guarantee for policies with a common continuation. This is a close planning prior, so changing dependence or reversing variance reduction is not alone a new security concept. The present construction instead preserves whole forecast-batch laws while choosing bank-dependent permutations to alter CEM rankings; novelty remains to be established.
  • An Omitted Mode Is a Rare Rule: The Sampling-Verification Danger Law in Continuous Code World Models：Primary abstract and omission/acceptance propositions inspected. The mechanism concerns sampled gates missing rare rules and planners exploiting a model that differs on those rules. The current coupling candidate preserves each candidate forecast law, so it does not rely on a rare omitted transition. This operational difference is not itself proof of novelty.
  • Reparameterization Gradient for Non-differentiable Models：Primary abstract and estimator description inspected. Pathwise differentiation alone can omit boundary contributions in nondifferentiable models; the paper adds boundary terms to obtain unbiased gradients. The current action-dependent quantile-wrap witness relies on this established issue. A native world-model security contribution remains unproven; do not claim missing-boundary bias as a new theorem.
  • Diffusion Model Predictive Control：Primary method and section4.3 inspected: separate diffusion action proposals and multi-step dynamics; explicit acausal joint trajectory prediction is contrasted with autoregressive conditionals. No local reproduction. The inspected paper did not supply an official checkpoint/code link; this is a bounded availability check, not proof none exists.
  • Do Differentiable Simulators Give Better Policy Gradients?：Exact simulation gradients can be biased under discontinuities; stiff smooth approximations can have finite-sample empirical bias. Generic truthful-forward/wrong-gradient control failure is established.
  • Model-based Reinforcement Learning with Scalable Composite Policy Gradient Estimators：TPX combines likelihood-ratio and reparameterization gradients in Dreamer; correct-gradient alternatives and gradient variance control are existing baselines.
  • Does "Do Differentiable Simulators Give Better Policy Gradients?" Give Better Policy Gradients?：Current work revisits estimator switching and variance control; abstract inspected, not reproduced.
  • Fooling LIME and SHAP: Adversarial Attacks on Post hoc Explanation Methods：Primary abstract, paper and official implementation inspected. An OOD detector distinguishes real examples from explainer perturbations and routes behavior. Merely recognizing query origin and conditionally changing outputs is not a new security operation. No reproduction.
  • TRAP: Tail-aware Ranking Attack for World-Model Planning：Primary abstract describes a trigger-conditioned world-model backdoor targeting decision-critical imagined-trajectory ranking, with dual gates and experiments on DreamerV3 and TD-MPC2. Generic corruption of imagined-trajectory ranking already has a security prior. The abstract alone does not establish a parameter-free inference-only attack; no local reproduction.
  • A Control Theory of Predictability in Latent World Models：Primary theory and experimental passages distinguish data-supported error from planner-reachable off-manifold error, including Jacobian amplification, non-normal transients, and nilpotent operators. Generic recursive amplification or off-manifold sensitivity is not a new contribution. No local reproduction.
  • When Backdoors Go Beyond Triggers: Semantic Drift in Diffusion Models Under Encoder Attacks：Primary abstract inspected. Encoder poisoning can cause trigger-free semantic corruption and low-rank sensitivity deformations. A generic trigger-free low-rank representation drift claim is insufficient novelty; full methods not verified or reproduced.
  • LeWorldModel: Stable End-to-End Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture from Pixels：Official repository commit8edfeb336732b5f3ce7b8b210d0ba370a09e2cac inspected: JEPA criterion scores terminal squared latent goal distance and get_cost re-encodes the goal. Evaluation freezes weights. Only the native criterion was exercised on constructed states; no pretrained model task experiment.
  • The Objective Is the Bottleneck: Latent World Models Encode What Their Planners Cannot Use：Primary methods inspected: frozen LeWM embeddings support position and temporal-distance objectives that improve TwoRoom planning. Prediction quality alone does not establish useful Euclidean goal geometry. These objective-only repairs are required comparators for geometry attacks, not a new mechanism supplied by our coordinate wrapper.
  • ATLAS: Aligned Transport of Latent Structure for Reliable World Model Planning：Primary section3 inspected: Gaussian marginal calibration and pairwise relational preservation are nonredundant; finite-candidate planning stability depends on relational distortion, scale, and prediction error. Preserving a Gaussian marginal while changing goal distances alone does not distinguish our candidate.
  • Anisotropic Representations Improve Planning in JEPA World Models：Primary theoretical sections inspected: the induced metric depends on data covariance and prediction noise, and anisotropic target geometry can improve planning with the same Euclidean planner. Exact encoder/predictor reparameterization plus altered metric needs a separate security operation and native evidence.
  • WorldAgen: Unified State-Action Prediction with Test-Time World Model Training：Proposes a shared Transformer with mixed attention masks separating task-conditioned action prediction and task-agnostic observation prediction, plus observation-loss LoRA adaptation. Our pinned code/weight probe finds an indirect goal-to-input-action-to-world path; no native task-harm or benchmark reproduction is claimed.
  • Grounding Spatial Relations in a Compact World Model: Instruction Leakage and a Goal-Free Dynamics Fix：Shows instruction transcription when text names the scored quantity, tests counterfactual/withheld instructions in tabletop, BabyAI, and Language-Table, and removes goal conditioning from dynamics. It reports no direct probe of a released language-conditioned pretrained world model. Our released-weight result is an implementation-level extension, not a new broad mechanism by itself.
  • JEPA-TTT: Persistent Test-Time Training of Latent World Models for Planning under Dynamics Shifts：Adapts predictor/action components with real observation and executed-action windows; visual encoder and reward head remain frozen. Dense updates and replay persist over deployment. Arbitrary prediction queries are not adaptation records, so it does not establish a query-only contamination operation.
  • Deep Reinforcement Learning in a Handful of Trials using Probabilistic Dynamics Models：PETS distinguishes within-model randomness from ensemble disagreement, persistent model identities from per-step resampling, and Gaussian moment matching from distribution sampling. These operations change multi-step uncertainty and can affect control; relabeling them as an uncertainty-source operation alone supplies no new mechanism.
  • Adversarial Attacks Against Uncertainty Quantification：Targets uncertainty estimates regardless of prediction correctness, including downstream overconfidence and underconfidence; minimum-variance and stabilizing input attacks are evaluated for classification and segmentation. It does not establish exact predictive-law preservation for our Gaussian world-model construction.
  • World Action Verifier: Self-Improving World Models via Forward-Inverse Asymmetry：Primary method uses generated subgoals to prioritize acquisition, then trains with real environmental outcomes. Pinned MiniGrid default oracle collector executes the recorded dataset action, while its model mode uses inferred actions and synthetic next states. Our one-step goal selector is a new composition, not that acquisition method. Inverse-only failure would not establish defeat of the full reverse-cycle method.
  • ACID: Action Consistency via Inverse Dynamics for Planning with World Models：Sections3.2–3.3 and AppendixC/E define goal cost plus inverse-action residual with a coefficient lambda*std(goal_cost)/std(action_cost), recomputed over every CEM pool. This supplies a possible cross-candidate path; no attack or full ACID reproduction is established by our categorical MiniGrid selector.
  • A Range-Normalization Model of Context-Dependent Choice: A New Model and Evidence：The 2012 primary article explicitly considers decoys dominated on every attribute and models changes to preferences between other options through range normalization. The broad claim that a never-chosen alternative changes decisions through normalization is therefore not novel; an adversarial world-model result would require substantive additional mechanism and task evidence.
  • CycleGAN, a Master of Steganography：Shows generators can retain recoverable input information in imperceptible output signals while meeting cycle consistency; connects the effect to adversarial examples. This limits an earlier contemplated inverse-verifier blind-spot argument. No CycleGAN experiment performed.
  • The Model Plants the Trigger: Answer-Side Backdoor Attacks in Multi-Turn Large Language Models：The model produces an answer-side trigger that is subsequently consumed as dialogue history. Self-generated rather than user-inserted activation is therefore already an explicit attack mechanism; changing the carrier to a world-model latent would require a substantive additional property and native task evidence. No implementation or model modification from this paper was used.
  • LePlanner: An Iterative Amortized Controller For World Models：Explicitly identifies horizon-reset procrastination and proposes an arrival-and-hold objective. Describing indefinite postponement of goal arrival alone would not establish a new world-model security mechanism.
  • The Value Equivalence Principle for Model-Based Reinforcement Learning：Primary abstract and PDF sections2–3 inspected: models are equivalent relative to specified policy and value-function classes; enlarging those classes shrinks the equivalence set. Our proposed preservation under one stochastic prior is not a new general equivalence principle and does not imply control equivalence. No reproduction.
  • Unified World Models: Coupling Video and Action Diffusion for Pretraining on Large Robotic Datasets：Official repository cloned and read. Native process_batch with obs_num_frames2/action_len16/num_frames19 uses observations0–1, actions1–16, and targets17–18. Both targets follow the action segment, so this released configuration does not expose an earlier predicted frame to a later supplied action. LIBERO pretraining checkpoint linked; no weights downloaded, model run, finetuning or task benchmark performed.
  • FLEX-WAM: Flexible Block-Causal World–Action Models for Long-Horizon Imagination and Planning：Primary sectionsI–II inspected: within-block bidirectional attention and configurable block sizes; joint policy/world training is regulated to preserve action responsiveness. The inspected version explicitly withholds code/checkpoints until acceptance. No local model, planning or attack experiment; architecture alone does not prove harmful retrocausal influence.
  • Optimal query complexity for private sequential learning against eavesdropping：An observer sees the learner queries without their responses and knows the query strategy. The paper studies private sequential learning and query-complexity bounds. Moving this observation surface to world-model planning alone is insufficient novelty.
  • Learner-Private Convex Optimization：Studies an eavesdropper observing iterative queries and obfuscation of the learned optimum under full-gradient feedback, with Bayesian and minimax guarantees. Generic private-goal leakage from adaptive planner queries is therefore not promoted as a new mechanism.
  • Privacy-Preserving Constrained Quadratic Optimization with Fisher Information：Section II derives noisy linear measurements of quadratic objective parameters from consecutive optimization iterates. It explicitly considers cost-parameter estimation, rather than only final-optimum inference. The proposed world-model query observer needs a substantive additional mechanism and native evidence.
  • BadWAM: When World-Action Models Dream Right but Act Wrong：Defines world-action drift attacks that shift executed actions while keeping imagined futures plausible, including an imagination-preserving attack. A future model-specific query-privacy claim must distinguish private-objective inference from this integrity attack.
  • Do Robotic World Models Really Follow Actions? WorldEcho and WorldSync：WorldEcho evaluates whether generated futures follow queried actions using matched simulator rollouts; WorldSync trains action coverage and intervention-effect supervision. Generic action-query fidelity or off-expert action failure is already directly studied.
  • JailWAM: Jailbreaking World Action Models in Robot Control：Introduces a WAM safety classification and closed-loop verification framework for visual trajectory attacks. A new claim needs a different security operation and native evidence, rather than another generic WAM attack benchmark.
  • What If, But Privately: Private Counterfactual Retrieval：Studies a user retrieving a counterfactual instance while hiding the feature vector from the institution. This further constrains a generic claim that unexecuted counterfactual requests expose private information; any world-model contribution needs a distinct native planning mechanism and evidence.
  • Correlated Trajectory Uncertainty for Adaptive Sequential Decision Making：Studies smooth correlated residual/function sampling for probabilistic neural-network world models, arguing that marginally calibrated but temporally non-smooth samples can miscalibrate multi-step uncertainty and affect downstream adaptive policies. Our persistent-residual screen therefore treats correlation itself as established prior art; any security claim must add a distinct adversarial operation and native task-loss evidence.
  • AD-WM: Action-Discriminative World Models for Counterfactual Model Predictive Control：Argues that factual transition prediction is insufficient for MPC and explicitly trains action-discriminative counterfactual consistency. A security claim about action contrast must distinguish this predictive-training objective and show an adversarial operation plus native task harm.
  • Overcoming Statistical Bias in Action-Controllable World Models：Constrains reference, inverse-action, zero-action and transformed-action rollouts to reduce multi-step counterfactual bias. The existence of this consistency objective limits novelty of generic action-order or branch-consistency claims.
  • Security of World-Model-Based Embodied AI: A Lifecycle of Threats, Defenses, and Evaluation：The lifecycle taxonomy treats trajectory ranking, feedback provenance, and imagined rollout integrity as explicit world-model security properties. A terminal-outcome ownership claim must therefore be narrower than generic ranking or feedback manipulation and needs a distinct operation plus native task evidence.
  • Hierarchical World Models：The model includes a termination prediction head and uses predicted termination to truncate imagined rollouts and value estimates. This supports testing the termination channel, but also means terminal routing alone is not a novelty result.
  • Robust Deep Reinforcement Learning against Adversarial Perturbations on State Observations：Defines state-adversarial MDPs in which an attacker changes observations without changing the underlying environment. A source-valid branch-splice result must show a world-model-specific effect beyond this observation attack class.
  • ActionSplice: In-Flight Action Editing for Interactive World Models：Introduces counterfactual state transport for in-flight action editing in chunk-autoregressive world models. A generic claim that stale or spliced internal state changes future predictions is already constrained; any security operation must differ and show native task harm.
  • State-Covering Trajectory Stitching for Diffusion Planners：Studies stitching short trajectory segments to expand planning coverage. This limits novelty of generic trajectory concatenation; an attack claim would need a distinct security boundary and native evidence.
  • Mind the Model, Not the Agent: The Primacy Bias in Model-based RL：Shows primacy bias in the learned world model of MBPO and DreamerV2 and proposes model resetting across MuJoCo, DMC and Atari. A legal-contributor or early-trajectory starvation claim must add a distinct security operation beyond ordinary early-data bias.
  • Safety, Security, and Cognitive Risks in World Models：Surveys world-model threats and reports trajectory-persistent adversarial attacks under adversarial fine-tuning on GRU/RSSM plus a DreamerV3 action-drift probe. Our action-only legal-interaction threat must remain distinct from gradient/data fine-tuning and should not claim generic trajectory persistence as novel.

原始 24 页方案保留，本文记录已发生的研究进展。

Goal-Bus 物理输出槽审计（历史记录，见页首校准边界）

在 AlphaBrain 的 21 个真实 image/state/recorded-action 窗口上，固定同一状态、16 步已执行动作、future target 与 sampler noise，Goal-Swap 改变 future proprio 槽 21/21，绝对 latent delta 0.0607，episode-cluster CI [0.0376, 0.0970]；fixed-action world cut 在 future proprio、wrist、primary 三个槽均 exact zero。扩展 legal offset 1/5/9 后共 63 个窗口，各 offset 的 future-proprio effect 仍 21/21 非零，均值 0.0512/0.0607/0.0728。target-error 方向仍分槽混合，fixed-action cut 还产生 wrist/primary fidelity cost，因此结论是“Goal 改写物理输出预测”，不是“Goal divergence 必然使物理误差增大”。机器结果；图；主报告。

AlphaBrain forecast-driven planner 可行性门（负结果）

为检查第二载体是否能把物理 future forecast 接入独立动作决策，固定 task 0/seed 17、8 条冻结候选、同一初始状态与 30-step LIBERO replay，用 future-primary forecast 的 CLIP 分数选动作，真实 EEF 进展只从环境读取。新增 CLIP 评分 Planner 的 Clean 臂选候选 4、EEF 距离 regret 0.0621 m；Goal-Swap 选候选 6、regret 0.4228 m；fixed-action Bus-Cut 让 Clean/Swap 分数逐项相同，但两者都选候选 6、regret 0.4228 m，因此没有恢复 Clean decision。这是新增 Planner 的单任务可行性及负修复结果；EEF 距离来自真实回放，但只是物理进展代理量，不能代替原生任务成功率。

机器结果；脚本；主报告。

历史同场景 Goal crossing：环境端固定动作回放控制

旧实验固定 LIBERO-Goal 的三个 simulator 初态和四组动作，对十个文本交叉回放，共 120 次。已记录的 sim/qpos/qvel、接触数量 ncon、三步图像及 EEF 字段均匹配；这不代表完整控制器状态或完整接触张量已经验证。同一实际状态的回放一致，不证明模型可见信息充分，也不证明训练分布中的 Goal 条件独立。任务语料库中的文本不自动构成目标模型同场景合法 Goal。

旧协议记录 native Goal effect 为 0.4913、Bus-Cut 跨 Goal 方差最大 6.2e-15；target-MSE 差 +0.0965、区间 [+0.0615,+0.1260] 和 Bus-Cut +0.0884 仅为历史未校准读数，因目标编码问题停用，不作当前误差增加或修复代价证据。

旧原始分析；旧实验脚本；当前已校准对照。

AlphaBrain 三 task forecast-driven planner 扩展

在 task 0/2/4、seed 17 上固定每 task 8 条动作候选、future-primary 16-step forecast 和 30-step LIBERO replay。Goal-Swap 改变 2/3 个 task 的选中动作；Clean/Goal-Swap/Bus-Cut regret 均值为 0.1836/0.2251/0.2796 m，Clean/Swap cut 分数逐项恒等，Bus-Cut 只在 1/3 task 恢复 Clean action。task 4 的 Goal-Swap regret 从 0.3765 m 降到 0.1402 m，显示真实效用方向依赖 task/planner。该结果支持跨载体 planner reachability，不支持普遍任务损害。

机器结果；脚本；主报告。

AlphaBrain 六 task planner 主 pilot

将同一 forecast-driven planner 扩展到 WorldAgen 主 sweep 的 6 个 task（0/2/4/6/7/9），seed 17、每 task 8 个冻结候选、16-step forecast、30-step replay。Goal-Swap 改变 5/6 个选中动作；Clean/Swap/Bus-Cut regret 均值 0.1646/0.1911/0.2433 m；Bus-Cut 只恢复 2/6 个 Clean action，且 task 4/6 的效用方向改善、0/7/9 变差。结果支持跨载体 planner reachability，排除普遍 utility harm 和完整 decision repair。

机器结果；脚本；主报告。

AlphaBrain 六 task × 三显式初态复放

同一 planner 在 `init_index=0/1/2` 上复放，共 18 个 task-state rows；Goal-Swap 改变 15/18 个 selected action。Clean/Swap/Bus-Cut regret 均值为 0.1511/0.1885/0.2607 m，Swap−Clean +0.0374 m；初态聚类 bootstrap CI [+0.0098,+0.0758]，task 聚类 CI [−0.1086,+0.1931]。Bus-Cut 只恢复 3/18 个 Clean action，Clean/Swap cut scores 逐项恒等；跨状态有描述性扰动，但不能作普遍 task harm 或完整 repair 结论。

聚合结果；聚合脚本；主报告。

物理前提修订：干预不变性与条件预测分开

准确命题是：固定完整增广物理状态 s、覆盖全部预测时域的已承诺低层动作 a 与同一外生随机源 u；当查询 Goal 只表示偏好且不改变场景、控制、时长或其他主体行为时，Y(s, do(A=a), do(G=g₁), u) = Y(s, do(A=a), do(G=g₂), u)。这不等同于观察数据中的条件独立式；反馈策略选出的动作不能代替动作干预。Goal 仍可合理改变策略、价值和任务成功标准，但物理比较须保持执行时域固定。

模型若仅看到局部观测，Goal 可能帮助推断隐藏状态。WorldAgen 的可检验依据是其明确的指令隔离承诺；AlphaBrain／Cosmos 不继承这一承诺。旧回放与 target-MSE 不能补足训练支持或观测充分性的证据。

当前 v3 规范；完整前提与反例。

历史 empty-Goal 对照：准确性与先验推论已撤回

旧协议对 50 个初态、四组固定动作记录了 200 次回放；其 target-MSE 差 +0.0744、区间 [+0.0665,+0.0824] 和输出距离 0.2139 保留作来源记录。目标编码与图像处理未校准，因此不再以这些读数推断 Clean 文本携带“稳定任务先验”。空文本本身也可能偏离训练支持；它不是训练过的无 Goal 动力学模型，不能识别隐藏状态信息的作用。

旧机器记录；旧脚本；当前解释边界。
